Use to try a branch or PR without checking it out. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-pr-debug) · ★697)
- **fxa-pr-open**: Drafts an FXA pull request title and body following the repo PR template and team conventions, then opens the PR as a draft after explicit confirmation. Use when a feature branch is ready and the user wants to open a PR. Do not invoke for edits to an existing PR. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-pr-open) · ★697)
- **fxa-pr-status**: Lists open FXA PRs matching a search term with a rich status table — file/line counts, draft state, review activity, and approval status. Defaults to all open PRs needing review. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-pr-status) · ★697)
- **fxa-review**: Thorough FXA-specific commit review using parallel specialist agents. Covers security, TypeScript, logic/bugs, test quality, and architecture. Agents explore call sites, git history, and monorepo conventions. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-review) · ★697)
- **fxa-review-quick**: Fast single-pass FXA-specific commit review covering security, conventions, logic/bugs, tests, and migrations. No subagents — runs directly in the main context. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-review-quick) · ★697 · 2 copies)
- **fxa-security-review**: Comprehensive security review of changed code. Checks for OWASP vulnerabilities, secrets leakage, XSS, SQL/Redis injection, CORS issues, insecure crypto, OTP/TOTP misuse, insufficient logging, Docker/CI exposure, and more. Use when landing changes that touch auth, payments, user data, or any security-sensitive path. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-security-review) · ★697)
- **fxa-session-debug**: Use when a Slack agent session failed, stalled, or said "Something went wrong", or someone pastes the fxa-agent thread and asks what happened. Collects the session's record, boot and finish logs, recorded errors and last messages, and maps the symptom to a known cause and fix. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-session-debug))
- **fxa-simplify**: Simplifies and refines code in the FXA monorepo using project-specific conventions. Use when asked to simplify, clean up, or refine recently written code. Focuses on recently modified code unless instructed otherwise. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-simplify) · ★697)
- **fxa-stack**: Start, check, and fix the local FxA stack on the sandbox VM. Use before a functional test, a screenshot, or any check that needs auth, content, or settings running, and when a service is down or erroring. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-stack))
- **fxa-storybook-capture**: Use inside the FxA sandbox VM to screenshot the component states a ticket changed, using Storybook, and hand the files to the host for PR attachment. Trigger when the diff touches a component that has a sibling *.stories.tsx. Not for user flows across pages, which need the full stack. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-storybook-capture))
- **fxa-test-draft**: Drafts Jest tests for changed code. Defaults to staged/unstaged changes or the most recent commit. Output is a starting point for review, not final. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-test-draft) · ★697)
- **fxa-test-independence**: Validates that Jest tests in a given file pass both as a full suite and individually in isolation, catching hidden order dependencies and shared mutable state. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-test-independence) · ★697)
- **fxa-test-plan**: Use inside the FxA sandbox before you change code. Plan how you will prove each behavior the ticket changes, the way a user or client would see it (a functional flow, a direct check against the running stack, an integration spec), with unit tests for edge cases. Write it to /workspace/.fxa-test-plan.json and let /fxa-verify --plan run it. Use again when the plan changes. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-test-plan))
- **fxa-test-repair**: Reviews a test file for violations of FXA testing guidelines, then suggests concrete repairs. Does not auto-apply changes — output is a repair plan for engineer review. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-test-repair) · ★697)
- **fxa-triage**: Generates a daily FxA triage summary. Queries Jira, GitHub, Bugzilla, and optionally Sentry, then outputs a Slack-ready update with detailed analysis. Use when starting triage duties or preparing the daily triage post. Also offers a dependency alert & bump PR review run that invokes the fxa-dep-triage skill. ([mozilla/fxa](https://github.com/mozilla/fxa/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/fxa-triage) · ★697)
- **fxa-unslop**: Use inside the FxA sandbox on your own change before the handoff. Checks the diff, the tests and the PR body for what reviewers of FxA agent PRs flag again and again, and fixes it. Run after /code-simplifier and /ponytail-review, and again on the PR body after /humanizer. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-unslop))
- **fxa-verify**: Verify a change in the FxA monorepo on the sandbox VM with the fastest correct checks. Maps each changed file to its package and runs only the related unit tests, lint, Prettier (it formats them) and a type-check on the changed files, then prints a verdict table. Use before saying tests pass, and before the handoff. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-verify))
- **fxa-vm-handoff**: Use inside the FxA sandbox VM to write /workspace/.fxa-auto-done.json, the handoff file the host reads to open the pull request. Run it last, after the PR body is drafted. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-vm-handoff))
- **fxa-vm-selfcheck**: Use inside the FxA sandbox VM after /fxa-review-quick and before the handoff. ([vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl](https://github.com/vbudhram/fxa-sandbox-ctl/tree/HEAD/skills/fxa-vm-selfcheck))
- **fxai-ad-video**: 广告视频（广告片）制作管线（用户级）。当用户说「做一条广告 / 广告片 / 广告视频 / 宣传片 / 产品视频 / 招商片 / 种草片 / 产品演示 / 10 秒广告 / 品牌片 / 旅游大片」时使用。覆盖：立项（片型·时长·卖点）→ 创意方向与机制 → 口播稿与分镜 → 素材（场景图 / 参考图 / 既有素材）→ 配音与节拍 → 后期包装（标题卡 / 信息贴片 / 片尾品牌卡 / 字幕 / 封面）→ 组装交付。四条路线：产品演示片、品牌蒙太奇、静图运镜旅拍、氛围片。不做：短剧走 fxai-short-drama、MV 走 fxai-mv-pipeline、纯生图走 fxai-comfyui-image-gen、纯数字人口播走 fxai-digital-human-video。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-ad-video) · ★3)

- **fxai-face-edit**: 人脸替换 / 修复 / 增强 / 整片角色替换：换脸、唇形同步、老照片修复上色、人脸增强、背景移除、整片视频人脸精修（本机 ComfyUI 分段链）、整片视频换人换装（MiniMax H3 分段角色替换，保留原动作与运镜）、全场景替换（人、场景、道具、配乐整套换掉）。当用户要求「把 A 的脸换成 B」「给这段视频换脸」「让这个人说这段话」「人脸更清晰一点」「整条片子精修一遍」「把这个视频里的人换成参考图那个人」「角色替换」「换掉主角」「全场景替换 / 换全部场景 / 场景道具都换掉」「主体替换 / 仅替换主体 / 只换主体」「删掉画面里某个人」「去掉另一个人」「只留一位」「删除指定人物」时使用。三条链路：FaceFusion REST（云端，换脸 / 对嘴 / 单图）、本机分段精修（只修脸）、本机分段角色替换（H3 换人 LoRA，身份与服装一起换）。只按地址发 HTTP，不启停服务。不做：生图配图（→fxai-comfyui-image-gen）、数字人口播（→fxai-digital-human-video）、建实例与开关机（→fxai-xiangongyun-deploy）。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-face-edit) · ★3)

- **fxai-h3-video-prompt**: MiniMax Hailuo H3 视频提示词写作规范（用户级，通用）。覆盖我们用到的两条分支——【首尾帧 FL2VA + 音频参考】（单场景/多场景）与【全参考 Ref2VA + 音频参考】（多参考），并强调"音频=原音频、不另行设计音轨"、"提示词必须契合文案内容与场景（含道具）"；另有「中文对白镜头（短剧分镜）写法硬规范」，覆盖站位/距离/视线/谁对谁说话/台词归属/运镜顺序。需要为自建 MiniMax 数字人 MV / 数字人口播视频写或审 H3 提示词、写/审短剧分镜提示词、排查人物/场景不一致或镜头乱动时使用。MV 与数字人口播共用这一套规范；配合 fxai-audio-video / fxai-mv-pipeline / fxai-digital-human-video / fxai-short-drama 使用；**打斗镜另需先向 fxai-combat-design 取招式术语与物理短语**（取词契约见 `references/05` / `06` 册与铁律 11）；**触发词（Combat V2 / Camera Motion LoRA）挂载见 `references/07` 册与铁律 13**——短剧管线 `build_lines_data(auto_triggers=True)` 已按镜型自动挂载，MV / 口播走 `build_ref2va_prompt… ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-h3-video-prompt) · ★3)
- **fxai-image-to-jpeg**: 把 PNG/源图转成最高质量 JPEG（quality=95 + subsample=0/4:4:4 + optimize=True），用于公众号/文章配图、上传前压体积。跨项目公共技能，纯 PIL 实现，不依赖任何项目本地路径。当用户说"转 JPEG / 压成高清图 / 图片转 JPG 上传 / 公众号配图转格式"时使用。；不做：不做设计与排版，只转格式。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-image-to-jpeg) · ★3)
- **fxai-infocard**: 给任意视频叠加「信息贴片」——半透明形状（卡片/气泡/胶囊/便签/绶带/尖标签/书签/纸签/下划线/括号）的辅助性补充内容层，按时间码出现在画面任意位置，带淡入/滑入/弹入 + 停留 + 淡出。当用户要求「加信息贴片 / 贴个说明 / 加补充提示 / 名词解释卡 / 加提示卡片 / 补充信息卡 / 加对话气泡 / 加关键词胶囊 / 把补充说明贴上去」时使用。与产线无关，任何视频都能叠。不负责字幕（fxai-lyric-subtitle）、不负责标题卡（fxai-title-card）、不负责封面。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-infocard) · ★3)
- **fxai-lyric-subtitle**: (视频字幕) 给 MV / 视频加标准歌词字幕。当用户说"给视频加字幕 / 歌词字幕 / 字幕烧录 / 卡拉OK 字幕 / 给 MV 加歌词"时使用。覆盖：whisper 提取时间戳 → 歌词文字校正 → 生成 SRT/ASS → 用带 libass 的 ffmpeg 烧录。全程本地处理，不上传视频。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-lyric-subtitle) · ★3)
- **fxai-material-collect**: 素材采集。给一条网页链接，把页面上的视频与图集取回本地，供分镜拆解、角色替换、剪辑使用。当用户说「扒下来 / 抓取视频 / 下载这个链接 / 采集素材 / 把这条存到本地 / 按链接取素材」时使用；只负责取素材，不负责剪辑、转码、换脸与发布。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-material-collect) · ★3)
- **fxai-mixed-layout**: fxai 混排视频的公共件（**公共目录，不是技能，没有可触发的能力**）。装的是「版式槽 + 素材槽」：槽里可以填图片（单图 / 多图轮播）、视频片段、或一整页网页（文字卡 / 长图滚动 / 真网页 / 自定义 HTML），最后都归一成一条同尺寸的无声视频流，交给外层去叠壳、叠字幕、拼接。被 fxai-slide-video（上中下分栏等版式）、fxai-ad-video（广告片的混排段）、fxai-poster（网页模式）自动 import。当需要「加一种新的展示形式」「改素材槽行为」「查版式登记表」时读这里；普通用户不需要直接调用它。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-mixed-layout) · ★3)
- **fxai-mv-pipeline**: 音乐→图片→MV 一体化生成管线（用户级公共能力）。当用户说「做首歌出个 MV / 生成音乐并配视频 / 用 MiniMax 生成数字人 MV / 单场景·多场景·多参考 MV / 给我做个音乐短视频」时使用。覆盖：音乐生成（Yue2 默认 / MiniMax Music 3 支持男女对唱）→ 千问2.1 生成人物与场景图 → MiniMax 三种模式（单场景/多场景/多参考）MV 视频生成，所有提示词中文。不做：口播视频（走 fxai-digital-human-video）、短剧（走 fxai-short-drama）、纯生图（走 fxai-comfyui-image-gen）、只生歌不出片（走 fxai-yue2）。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-mv-pipeline) · ★3)

- **fxai-outro-card**: 生成并拼接「片尾卡」——视频结尾那一张品牌收束卡（Logo + 品牌名 + 标语，可选联系信息）。按成片宽高自适应横竖屏，输出可直接拼接的 PNG 或已接好片尾的成片。当用户说「加片尾 / 加片尾卡 / 做个片尾 / 结尾加个品牌卡 / 后面接一个我们的卡 / 把原片尾卡换成我们的 / 加仙宫云那版片尾」时使用。本技能是**按需能力**，常规交付不自动生成；片头卡走 fxai-title-card，画面补充层走 fxai-infocard，封面走海报能力。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-outro-card) · ★3)
- **fxai-physical-publish**: 物理仿真发布通道。用系统级 SendInput 鼠标键盘操作真实浏览器窗口完成发布，不接管 CDP、不注入脚本、不读写 cookie 与登录态；首次录制站点操作区域，之后按区域真实点击，轨迹与间隔每次随机。适用于要求「完全像真人手动操作」的视频/图文/文章发布，或对自动化行为敏感的站点。触发词：物理仿真发布、真人操作发布、真实点击发布、模拟真人发布。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-physical-publish) · ★3)
- **fxai-poster**: 图片设计（全类型：视频封面 / 海报 / 文章配图 / 社交图 / 电商图 / 信息图 / 视频内嵌图）。主线是 AI 生图打底再叠文案层：hybrid（默认，生图+叠字）/ image（纯画面）/ web（中文须精确或大段文字）；类型与必备要素见 `08` 册，三道质检门（规格预检 / 提示词要素 / 成图指标）见 `09` 册。封面另有 9 种样式（含竖排、奶油胶囊卡），按类别自动选也可指定（`cover_styles.py`，`--list-styles` 查表），产物 PNG 可直接铺到视频首帧。不做：视频本体生成（→fxai-audio-video / fxai-mv-pipeline / fxai-short-drama）、纯配音（→fxai-voiceover）。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-poster) · ★3)

- **fxai-short-drama**: 短剧自动化生成管线（用户级，可分享整合包）。当用户说「做一集短剧/自动生成短剧/用双采版工作流出短剧/出新一集/批量生成多镜头短剧/短剧封面物料」时使用。流程：剧本→分镜→**（打斗镜先向 fxai-combat-design 取招式术语）**→角色四视图+场景图（复用 fxai-fourview-gen/fxai-scene-gen）→H3六段式对白提示词（**打斗镜触发词由 build_lines_data 自动挂载**）→组装 lines_data→提交双采版 Ref2VA 工作流批量生成→自动合并→字幕烧录→封面交付。不做：MV/唱歌视频（→fxai-mv-pipeline）、口播配音（→fxai-digital-human-video）、纯生图（→fxai-comfyui-image-gen）、剧本创作（→fxai-short-drama-script）、多平台发布与各站点封面派生（→fxai-browser-publish）。 ([fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills](https://github.com/fxai666/fxai-fully-automated-creation-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/fxai-short-drama) · ★3)
